\"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                             \\\n    do {                                                                                             \\\n        const cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                            \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__)); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                      \\\n        }                                                                                            \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA 7-point stencil\n//\n// Each thread block owns a BX x BY tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping\n// the z-1 / z+1 neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in\n// shared memory so that the x/y neighbours are read only once from memory.\n//\n// The domain boundary is never written by the stencil: the original code copies\n// the input boundary to the output on every iteration, and since the boundary\n// of the initial grid never changes, seeding both device buffers with the\n// initial grid once makes every later iteration produce identical results.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int SH_X = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SH_Y = BY + 2;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = sum / Real(7.0);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx - 2 + BX - 1) / BX);\n        const int iy = static_cast<int>((ny - 2 + BY - 1) / BY);\n        const int iz = static_cast<int>(nz - 2);\n        // Enough blocks to saturate the device while keeping the z-reuse long.\n        zChunk = 32;\n        while (zChunk > 1 && static_cast<long long>(ix) * iy * ((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk) < 2048) {\n            zChunk /= 2;\n        }\n        grid = dim3(ix, iy, static_cast<unsigned>((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Seed both buffers so the (invariant) boundary is correct in either one.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, grid1.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dB : dA;\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, inx, iny, inz, zChunk);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Result lives in dA when an even number of sweeps ran, dB otherwise.\n    Real* result = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n    std::vector<Real>& host = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data(), result, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    elapsedSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA 7-point stencil\n//\n// Each thread block owns a BX x BY tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping\n// the z-1 / z+1 neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in\n// shared memory so that the x/y neighbours are read only once from memory.\n//\n// The domain boundary is never written by the stencil: the original code copies\n// the input boundary to the output on every iteration, and since the boundary\n// of the initial grid never changes, seeding both device buffers with the\n// initial grid once makes every later iteration produce identical results.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int SH_X = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SH_Y = BY + 2;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = sum / Real(7.0);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx - 2 + BX - 1) / BX);\n        const int iy = static_cast<int>((ny - 2 + BY - 1) / BY);\n        const int iz = static_cast<int>(nz - 2);\n        // Enough blocks to saturate the device while keeping the z-reuse long.\n        zChunk = 32;\n        while (zChunk > 1 && static_cast<long long>(ix) * iy * ((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk) < 2048) {\n            zChunk /= 2;\n        }\n        grid = dim3(ix, iy, static_cast<unsigned>((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Seed both buffers so the (invariant) boundary is correct in either one.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, grid1.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dB : dA;\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, inx, iny, inz, zChunk);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Result lives in dA when an even number of sweeps ran, dB otherwise.\n    Real* result = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n    std::vector<Real>& host = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data(), result, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    elapsedSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA 7-point stencil\n//\n// Each thread block owns a BX x BY tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping\n// the z-1 / z+1 neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in\n// shared memory so that the x/y neighbours are read only once from memory.\n//\n// The domain boundary is never written by the stencil: the original code copies\n// the input boundary to the output on every iteration, and since the boundary\n// of the initial grid never changes, seeding both device buffers with the\n// initial grid once makes every later iteration produce identical results.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int SH_X = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SH_Y = BY + 2;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = sum / Real(7.0);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx - 2 + BX - 1) / BX);\n        const int iy = static_cast<int>((ny - 2 + BY - 1) / BY);\n        const int iz = static_cast<int>(nz - 2);\n        // Enough blocks to saturate the device while keeping the z-reuse long.\n        zChunk = 32;\n        while (zChunk > 1 && static_cast<long long>(ix) * iy * ((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk) < 2048) {\n            zChunk /= 2;\n        }\n        grid = dim3(ix, iy, static_cast<unsigned>((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Seed both buffers so the (invariant) boundary is correct in either one.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, grid1.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dB : dA;\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, inx, iny, inz, zChunk);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Result lives in dA when an even number of sweeps ran, dB otherwise.\n    Real* result = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n    std::vector<Real>& host = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data(), result, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    elapsedSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; r = fma(-7, q0, x) is exact, so x/7 = q0 + r/7\n// exactly, and fma(r, c, q0) rounds q0 + (r/7)(1+d), |d| <= u, i.e. a value within\n// a relative u^2 of x/7. Since 7 is odd, x/7 is never closer than ~2^-53 ulp to a\n// rounding midpoint, so the single rounding of the fma yields RN(x/7) exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBy7(const Real x) {\n    constexpr Real c = 1.0 / 7.0;  // RN(1/7)\n    const Real q0 = __dmul_rn(x, c);\n    const Real r = __fma_rn(-7.0, q0, x);\n    return __fma_rn(r, c, q0);\n}\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = divideBy7(sum);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx -                                                            \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__)); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                      \\\n        }                                                                                            \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA 7-point stencil\n//\n// Each thread block owns a BX x BY tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping\n// the z-1 / z+1 neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in\n// shared memory so that the x/y neighbours are read only once from memory.\n//\n// The domain boundary is never written by the stencil: the original code copies\n// the input boundary to the output on every iteration, and since the boundary\n// of the initial grid never changes, seeding both device buffers with the\n// initial grid once makes every later iteration produce identical results.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int SH_X = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SH_Y = BY + 2;\n\n// Correctly rounded x / 7.0 without an FP64 divide (which is emulated by a long\n// instruction sequence on consumer GPUs and dominates this kernel).\n//\n// With c = RN(1/7): q0 = RN(x*c); r = fma(-7, q0, x) is exact, so x/7 = q0 + r/7\n// exactly, and fma(r, c, q0) rounds q0 + (r/7)(1+d), |d| <= u, i.e. a value within\n// a relative u^2 of x/7. Since 7 is odd, x/7 is never closer than ~2^-53 ulp to a\n// rounding midpoint, so the single rounding of the fma yields RN(x/7) exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBy7(const Real x) {\n    constexpr Real c = 1.0 / 7.0;  // RN(1/7)\n    const Real q0 = __dmul_rn(x, c);\n    const Real r = __fma_rn(-7.0, q0, x);\n    return __fma_rn(r, c, q0);\n}\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = divideBy7(sum);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx - 2 + BX - 1) / BX);\n        const int iy = static_cast<int>((ny - 2 + BY - 1) / BY);\n        const int iz = static_cast<int>(nz - 2);\n        // Enough blocks to saturate the device while keeping the z-reuse long.\n        zChunk = 32;\n        while (zChunk > 1 && static_cast<long long>(ix) * iy * ((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk) < 2048) {\n            zChunk /= 2;\n        }\n        grid = dim3(ix, iy, static_cast<unsigned>((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk));\n    }\n\n    // Page-lock the host buffers so the PCIe transfers run at full speed.\n    std::vector<Real>& hostResult = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    const bool pinnedIn = (cudaHostRegister(grid1.data(), bytes, cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess);\n    const bool pinnedOut = (&hostResult == &grid1) ? false\n                                                   : (cudaHostRegister(hostResult.data(), bytes, cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess);\n    cudaGetLastError();  // clear a potential registration failure\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Seed both buffers so the (invariant) boundary is correct in either one.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, grid1.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dB : dA;\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, inx, iny, inz, zChunk);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Result lives in dA when an even number of sweeps ran, dB otherwise.\n    Real* result = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n    std::vector<Real>& host = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data(), result, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    elapsedSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1;                                                            \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__)); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                      \\\n        }                                                                                            \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA 7-point stencil\n//\n// Each thread block owns a BX x BY tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping\n// the z-1 / z+1 neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in\n// shared memory so that the x/y neighbours are read only once from memory.\n//\n// The domain boundary is never written by the stencil: the original code copies\n// the input boundary to the output on every iteration, and since the boundary\n// of the initial grid never changes, seeding both device buffers with the\n// initial grid once makes every later iteration produce identical results.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 4;\nstatic constexpr int SH_X = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SH_Y = BY + 2;\n\n// Correctly rounded x / 7.0 without an FP64 divide (which is emulated by a long\n// instruction sequence on consumer GPUs and dominates this kernel).\n//\n// With c = RN(1/7): q0 = RN(x*c); r = fma(-7, q0, x) is exact, so x/7 = q0 + r/7\n// exactly, and fma(r, c, q0) rounds q0 + (r/7)(1+d), |d| <= u, i.e. a value within\n// a relative u^2 of x/7. Since 7 is odd, x/7 is never closer than ~2^-53 ulp to a\n// rounding midpoint, so the single rounding of the fma yields RN(x/7) exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBy7(const Real x) {\n    constexpr Real c = 1.0 / 7.0;  // RN(1/7)\n    const Real q0 = __dmul_rn(x, c);\n    const Real r = __fma_rn(-7.0, q0, x);\n    return __fma_rn(r, c, q0);\n}\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ in, Real* __restrict__ out, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int nz, const int zChunk) {\n    __shared__ Real plane[SH_Y][SH_X];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n\n    // Origin of the halo region staged in shared memory.\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n\n    // Interior point owned by this thread.\n    const int x = x0 + tx + 1;\n    const int y = y0 + ty + 1;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    const long long slice = static_cast<long long>(nx) * ny;\n\n    const int zBegin = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zChunk + 1;\n    int zEnd = zBegin + zChunk;\n    if (zEnd > nz - 1) zEnd = nz - 1;\n    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;\n\n    const long long xyBase = static_cast<long long>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window in z.\n    Real below = active ? in[(zBegin - 1) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n    Real center = active ? in[static_cast<long long>(zBegin) * slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n    // Cooperative halo load of one z-plane into shared memory.\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int nThreads = BX * BY;\n\n    for (int z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n        const long long planeBase = static_cast<long long>(z) * slice;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int i = tid; i < SH_X * SH_Y; i += nThreads) {\n            const int lx = i % SH_X;\n            const int ly = i / SH_X;\n            int gx = x0 + lx;\n            int gy = y0 + ly;\n            if (gx > nx - 1) gx = nx - 1;\n            if (gy > ny - 1) gy = ny - 1;\n            plane[ly][lx] = in[planeBase + static_cast<long long>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const Real above = active ? in[planeBase + slice + xyBase] : Real(0);\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = plane[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = plane[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = plane[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = plane[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Same summation order as the reference implementation.\n            const Real sum = center + left + right + front + back + below + above;\n            out[planeBase + xyBase] = divideBy7(sum);\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\n// Host-side driver: runs `iterations` stencil sweeps on the device.\nstatic void runStencilGPU(std::vector<Real>& grid1, std::vector<Real>& grid2, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                          const int iterations, double& elapsedSeconds) {\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);\n\n    Real* dA = nullptr;\n    Real* dB = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, bytes));\n\n    const int inx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int iny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int inz = static_cast<int>(nz);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2) && (ny > 2) && (nz > 2);\n\n    dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    dim3 grid(1, 1, 1);\n    int zChunk = 1;\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        const int ix = static_cast<int>((nx - 2 + BX - 1) / BX);\n        const int iy = static_cast<int>((ny - 2 + BY - 1) / BY);\n        const int iz = static_cast<int>(nz - 2);\n        // Enough blocks to saturate the device while keeping the z-reuse long.\n        zChunk = 32;\n        while (zChunk > 1 && static_cast<long long>(ix) * iy * ((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk) < 2048) {\n            zChunk /= 2;\n        }\n        grid = dim3(ix, iy, static_cast<unsigned>((iz + zChunk - 1) / zChunk));\n    }\n\n    // Page-lock the host buffers so the PCIe transfers run at full speed.\n    std::vector<Real>& hostResult = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    const bool pinnedIn = (cudaHostRegister(grid1.data(), bytes, cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess);\n    const bool pinnedOut = (&hostResult == &grid1) ? false\n                                                   : (cudaHostRegister(hostResult.data(), bytes, cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess);\n    cudaGetLastError();  // clear a potential registration failure\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Seed both buffers so the (invariant) boundary is correct in either one.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, grid1.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    if (hasInterior) {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? dB : dA;\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, inx, iny, inz, zChunk);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Result lives in dA when an even number of sweeps ran, dB otherwise.\n    Real* result = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? dA : dB;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResult.data(), result, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    elapsedSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CekwfcPPxQY5hAiKc9GPi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark now runs on the GPU with CUDA, bit-exact against the original.\n\n## What changed\n\n**`stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp`** — now compiled as CUDA (kept the same filename, no new files):\n\n- **Kernel**: each block owns a 32×4 tile in (x,y) and marches along z, keeping the `z-1`/`z+1` neighbours in registers and staging the current z-plane in shared memory, so each cell is loaded from DRAM essentially once. z is chunked across `gridDim.z` so block count stays high on small grids.\n- **Boundary handling**: the reference copies input→output boundaries every iteration, but the boundary of the initial grid is invariant, so both device buffers are seeded once from the initial grid and the kernel only touches the interior. Same results, no per-iteration boundary pass.\n- **Exact summation order** (`center+left+right+front+back+bottom+top`) preserved — FP addition isn't associative and the harness hashes the bits.\n- **FP64 divide removed**: `/7.0` was ~50% of kernel time (consumer GPUs emulate FP64 division). Replaced with a 3-op FMA sequence that is *correctly rounded*: since `x/7` is never within a relative 2⁻¹⁰⁵ of a rounding midpoint (7 is odd), the u²-accurate pre-rounded value always rounds to `RN(x/7)`. Rationale is in a comment; I also fuzzed it against `x/7.0` over ~8.6 billion inputs (random bit patterns, dense binades, smallest normals) with zero mismatches.\n- **Transfers**: host buffers are page-locked with `cudaHostRegister` before the timed region. H2D/D2H are *included* in the reported time.\n- `iterations <= 0` short-circuits so grid2 stays untouched, matching the original.\n\n**`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt`** — CUDA language enabled, `stencil3d.cpp` tagged `LANGUAGE CUDA`, `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, host flags forwarded via `-Xcompiler`. Executable name unchanged.\n\n## Correctness\n\nHash/sum/samples match the CPU reference bit-for-bit on 15+ configurations: cubic and non-cubic grids, odd/even/zero/negative iteration counts, degenerate sizes (`-x 1/2/3`, `-x 0`), and highly anisotropic shapes (`1024×8×8`, `8×1024×8`, `7×7×1000`).\n\n## Performance (RTX 3090, end-to-end incl. PCIe)\n\n| Case | CPU ref | CUDA | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 128³, 10 it | 513 MCUPS | ~5,300 | 10× |\n| 512³, 50 it | 429 MCUPS | 16,338 | 38× |\n| 512³, 100 it | 435 MCUPS | ~21,000 | 48× |\n\nKernel-only is 4.6 ms per 512³ sweep (29 GCell/s, ~470 GB/s effective). That's within ~15% of this GPU's hard floor for the workload: the required 9 FP64 instructions per cell cost ~3.9 ms at the GA102 FP64 issue rate of 1/64. Small-grid runs are PCIe-transfer-bound, and I measured shared-memory-tiled vs. cache-only variants and several block shapes — all within noise of each other, confirming the FP64 pipe, not memory or tiling, is the limit."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":486,"cache_read_input_tokens":70152,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":486},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9e50a59d-4a6a-49a3-b787-9814d13c002a","uuid":"070be2c7-e370-412e-8d72-ae8a0c895173","timestamp":"2026-09-05T20:43:43.294Z","request_id":"req_011CekwfbbGsRN3Hn7LFxF3N"}
